ṭiyeṅ camaveḷiyil catuppu nilattiṉ nili aṅkuc cellap paṉmuṟi aṭikkaṭi muyaṉṟum tiruptikaramāṉa vaḻi kiṭṭavilli. āṉāl, paṇṭikkāla intu cakāppattiltāṉ itaṟku nalla vaḻi kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭiruntatu. muṉṉark kuṭiyēṟi irunta iṭattilkūṭa iṉṉum nīrōṭik koṇṭē irukkiṟatu.
kaṭal maṭṭattiṉiṉṟu 6,500 aṭi uyarattil kaviṉ peṟa ilaṅkum ṭiyeṅ mēṭṭil, aṉṉiyarkaḷukku upayōkamillā mal taṉittup parantu kiṭakkum intak kōyil tokutikaḷ namatu uḷḷattik kavarak kūṭiya uyarnta iṉpan tarum iṭamākum. tattuvārttam tarum aintu vēṟuvēṟāṉa kōyil tokutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avikaḷil cila naṉku pātukākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ēṉiya vikaḷ veṟum kaṟkuviyalkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pañca pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ pukaḻmikka parampariyāl aṭikkaṭi ārōpikkum, intiyāvil ātiyil eḻunta paḻamperum kōyil eṉpatu ulakap piracitta peṟṟatākum. itaṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkuvatu ceṉṉik kaṭaṟkariyil eṭuppikkap peṟṟa oṟṟik kalkōyil tokutiyākum avi tarmarāj, pīma, arccuṉaṉ, cakātēva, avarkaḷiṉ potuvāṉa pattiṉiyāṉa turōpati ākiyavarkaḷiṉ iratamākap peyartarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi namatu cakāptattiṉ ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntavi eṉṟu kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil perum nipuṇarāy viḷaṅkum pēraṟiñar verkūcaṉ avarkaḷ moḻintuḷḷārkaḷ.1
tamiḻ nāṭṭil eḻuntuḷḷa itē eṇṇam cāvakattilum eḻuntu aintu kōyilākap pañcapāṇṭavarkaḷ peyaril kāṇap paṭuvatu āccariyappaṭuvataṟkuriya aruñ ceyalākak karuta muṭiyātu. cāvakattil utiṣṭirarukkuc cāṇṭi pāṇṭatēva eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. cāṇṭipīma, cāṇṭi arccaṉā eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyaruḷḷa kōyilkaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.
ṭiyeṅ mēṭu oru nākarikamāṉa talinakarākap pirati palikkavilli. āṉāl puṉitamāṉa teyvīka yāttirikkuriya iṭamāka miḷirntatu. itu kiṭṭattaṭṭa maturi, tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, kaṉṉiyākumari, tiruccentūr, cīrkāḻi, tirukkaṭiyūr, citamparam pōṉṟa puṉita iṭamākum. iṅka nirantaramākac camayap purōkitar kaḷum, kōyiṟ paṇiyāḷarkaḷum kuṭiyiruntārkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟip puṉita yāttirikkārarkaḷ taṅkum iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. āṇṭiṟ korumuṟi iṅkup pārviyiṭa varum maṉṉarum ēṉiyōrum taṅkuvataṟkut takka iṭaṅkaḷumuṇṭu. intak kōyilkaḷ ciṟiyaṉa. ovvoṉṟum cutantiramāṉatu. mikap periyaṉavaṟṟuḷ eṭṭuk kōyilkaḷ maṭṭum ippoḻutu uḷḷaṉa. potuvāka itaṉuṭiya tōṟṟam peṭṭi pōṉṟatu. allatu ucciyuḷḷatum, ceṅkuttāṉa oḻuṅku muṟiyip palamāka vaṟpuṟuttum kaṉacaturamāṉa kaṭṭumā ṉattiyuṭiyatumāy irukkiṟatu. ovvoru kōyilum, veḷic cuvariṉ orupakkattiliruntu talivāyil veḷiyē pitukkik koṇṭu niṟkum allatu oru vāyil maṇṭapattāl neruṅki irukkum ōr uṟutiyāṉa taṉi māṭam irukkum. ēṉiya mūṉṟu cuvarkaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟaṅkaḷiṉ mēṟpūccukaḷ mūṉṟu pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu mūṉṟu māṭaṅkaḷ allatu ciṟpa araṅkam veḷiyē turuttik koṇṭu irukkum. kūri piratāṉa māṭattiṉiṉṟu taṉatu amippit tirumpac ceykiṟatu. uṭpuṟam oru veḷiyāṉa kuḻalvaṭivamāṉa caricamattiṇmam uṭiyatāyk, kūriyiṉ uḷ tuḷiyuḷḷa kūmpuvaṭivap poruḷiṟkuk kīḻē, oru taṉik kallāl mūṭak kūṭiyatāṉa eñciya iṭaṅkaḷ varikkum uṭcuvarkaḷ neruṅki irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru vicittiramāṉa uruvan tāṅkiya kīrtti mukam (kalā makaramum, ulāntā āciriyarkaḷiṉ pāṉā pakutiyum) vāyiliyum, māṭaṅkaḷiyum aṇiceytu niṟkiṉṟatu. makaram apivirutti ceyyappaṭṭum, malar uruvaṅkaḷāl oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭum aritāṉateṉa aṟiñarkaḷāl aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭu muḷḷatu.
kalā makarā
kalā makarā eṉpatu vāyilkaḷiyum māṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṉpuṟaṅ kaḷiyum alaṅkarittuk kāṭṭa oru vicittiramāṉa vilaṅku vaṭivic ceṭikaḷālum koṭikaḷālum ceytu kaṭṭum ōr aḻakiya tōraṇamēyākum.
mēṟṟaḷam oṉṟiliruntu maṟṟoru mēṟṟaḷattiṉ vaḻiyāka mattiya ṭakōpāviṟkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ nāṉku tici tirumpāk kūṟāka mukkiyamāṉa kuṟiyāka irukkiṟatu. atu teḷi vākak kōyiliṉ puṉitap poruḷkaḷiṉ puṉitamāy irukkiṟatu. mēṟṟaḷattiṉ uṭpukavirukkum makattāṉa periya nuḻivāyil ovvoṉṟum aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. amippu muṟikaḷ evvāṟu vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum mattiya intiyāviṉ muṟkāla tūpaṅkaḷiṉ pukaḻpeṟṟa tōraṇaṅkaḷiṉ alaṅkarippu muṟiyi aṭuttup pōrōputūr, iṉṉum eṇṇaṟṟa kōyilkaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷiṉ mēl uḷḷa tōraṇa alaṅkarippu muṟi cantēkattiṟ kiṭamiṉṟi intiya mūlatti uṭiyatāka irukkiṉṟatu. mika vicittiramāṉa intap periya nuḻivāyilkaḷiṉ ciṟanta iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa talivāyil mēlē alaṅkāramāka amikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. atu payaṅkaramāṉa teyvamākiya kālāviṉ mātiri uruva māka matikkappaṭukiṟatu, intiyāvil inta alaṅkāra vēlip pāṭukaḷ ellām oru yāḷiyiṉ taliyi vittē aṇi ceyyap paṭum. tekkāṇattil uḷḷa tirāviṭak kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil iṉṉum yāḷiyiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu tirāviṭak kaṭṭiṭak kaliyippaṟṟi naṉku ārāynta ciṟpaviṟpaṉṉarum pirañcup pēraṟiñarumāṉa jāvē tuparē avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.1 ippoḻutu kālā tali eṉṟa kūṟappaṭum oru putiya uruvattic cāvakac ciṟpikaḷ cāvaka nāṭṭuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟilum iṇittu alaṅkarittuḷḷaṉar. iḵtu intiyāvil ciṟpikaḷ vāyilkaḷiṉ irupuṟamum vittu aṇi ceyyum makarattali pōl cāvakac ciṟpikaḷ itip payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.
kalā - makarā ciṟappuk kūṟu ōr iṇikkap peṟṟa alaṅkarippu eṉṟu ulāntā putiporuḷ ārāycci aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. iḵtu intō - cāvakak kaliyiṉ alaṅkaripput tiṭṭaṅkaḷil mikac ciṟantatākat tikaḻkiṟatu. intiyac ciṟpakkali yil kūṭa makaram ōr āpūrvamāṉa ciṟpap poruḷākak karutappaṭu kiṟatu. āṉāl ataṉuṭiya mūlat tōṟṟattip paṟṟi māṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷ aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. ataṉuṭiya curuṇṭa tutikki yāṉiyic cuṭṭuvatāka irukkum. āṉāl intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil makaram eṉpatu māṟṟak kūṭāta cāyal kāṭṭum nīril vāḻum oru mirukampōl irukkum. nām makara kālā mukāviṉ poḻutu pōkkukkuriya vinōtak katiyi niṉivukūra muṭikiṟatu. atōṭu pañcatantirak katikaḷil irākkatamukak kuraṅkiyum, puttarkaḷ paṭippiṉikkāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa cātakak katiyiyum niṉivupaṭutta muṭikiṟatu.2 nīril vāḻum makara mīṉiyum nilattil vāḻum yāṉiyiyum iṇittu oru vinōtamāṉa pirāṇiyic ciṟpavaṭivil kaṇṭatip pērāciriyar kuruṉ veṭal eṉpavar nīr yāṉi eṉṉum peyarāl kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.1 eṉṟālum makara uruvam muṉṉar intiyak kalikaḷil oru vilaṅ kākavē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu mutali vaṭivu oru vicēṭa alaṅkārap paṭuttappaṭṭu eṅkum evarum kaṇṭirāta oru putiya uruvam koṭuk kappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. vaṭa intiyāvil pīkāril uḷḷa lōma muṉi kukiyil makaram ataṉ paḻaṅkāla uruvil cittarikkappaṭṭirup pati nām pārkka muṭikiṟatu. intiyāviṉ paḻaṅkālak kukikaḷil kaṇṭa iyāṉa vaṭivamuḷḷa mirukattiṉiṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāka urumāṟṟamuṟṟu iṟutiyākac cāvakak kaliyiṉ vicittirap pāṇiyil aminta vicittirap paṭippuyirāka eḻuntatu. aḵtu aṭuttu oru putiya amippip peṟṟu apiviruttiyuṟṟut tōraṇa mākak kāṇappeṟṟatu. ittōraṇaṅkaḷ mattiya intiyāvil pārhūttiluḷḷa pukaḻ vāynta tūpāvil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. atiluḷḷa iru nuṉikaḷum mūvitamāṉa tūṇiṉ ucciyiluḷḷa paṟkaṭṭiyiṉ mītuḷḷa pakutiyil makara uruvattōṭu iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum aṉittum mutaliyiṉuṭiya mūlat taṉmikaḷ māṟāmalum aḻiyāmalum eñci niṟkiṉṟaṉa.2
eṉavē ivikaḷiyellām naṉkārāynta aṟiñar ṭākṭar. cē. piyec vōkal avarkaḷ tam nūlil potuvākac cāvakattil kāṇappaṭum kalā makara alaṅkāra vēlippāṭukaḷ niccayamāka intiyak kaliyic cārntaṉavē eṉpati uṟutip paṭuttiyuḷḷār. pōrōputūr puṉita ālayattiṉ periya nuḻi vāyiliṉmītu cittirikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kalāmakara tōraṇa vēlip pāṭukaḷ intiyac ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu māṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭiṉum intiya mūlavaṭiviṉiṉṟu apiviruttiyuṟṟuc cāvakap paṇpōṭu kalantuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟalām eṉa naṉku viḷakki irukkiṉṟār.3
intak kalāmakara tōraṇa ciṟpam mattiya cāvakattil mika mukkiyattuvam peṟṟatāka irukkiṟatu. itu kiḻakkuc cāvakattiṉ ciṉṉamākavum viḷaṅkiyatu. mattiya cāvakattilum kiḻakkuc cāvakat tilum kāṇappaṭum kalāmakara alaṅkāra vēlippāṭukaḷ aṭip paṭiyil cila vēṟupāṭukaḷiyuṭiyaṉa. mēlum kiḻakkuc cāvakat til atikamākak kāṇappaṭuvatilli. āṉāl aṅku itaṟku atika matippu aḷikkiṉṟaṉar.1
iṅku nām kāṭṭiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ cāvakattil uḷḷa arccuṉaṉ tokuti eṉṟa nāṉku kōyilkaḷum muṟiyē cāṇṭi arccuṉā, cirikāṇṭi, pāṇṭatēvā, cempatrā eṉappaṭum. cāṇṭi kāṭṭē kācā eṉappaṭuvatu nīṇṭa caturamuṭiya ciṟiya cāṇṭi cemar āka muṭiyātu. oruvēḷi ārampattil pokkica cāliyāka iruntu appāl arccuṉaṉ tokutiyil oru pākamāka iti amittiruk kalām. taṉittu oṟṟiyāka uyarntu niṟkum cāṇṭi pīmā ivikaḷiṉiṉṟu muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭatākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu. intak kaṭṭiṭattiṉ kīḻppakuti nām ēṟkeṉavē vivaritta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi yoppa irukkiṟatu. āṉāl kūri teḷivākap paṭṭik kūmpu vaṭivuṭiyatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. atu mēṟpūccōṭu aṭittaḷattiṉ amippu mutalilēyē tirumpat tirumpac ceyyappaṭṭu irukkiṟatu. ataṉuṭiya araṅkaṅkaḷ kuṟittuc ceṅkuttāṉatākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟiyavi taliyi oṭṭic cittiya palakaṇi pūvaṇi vēlikaḷ uṭiyaṉavāy alaṅkarikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa, allatu piratimikaḷ uyarnta ātaravuḷḷaṉavāy nāṉkāvatu āṟāvatu nilikaḷiṉ kōṇaṅkaḷāka mutirnta tāmarik kāykaḷāl nirappappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ōr aḻakiya taṉmiyil oru niṟivāṉa tāmarikkāy ucciyi irājarīkam ceykiṟatu. ivvāṟu kūri amippu intō - āriya cikara amippi oppa irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kali mētikaḷ cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. itu tañcāvūriluḷḷa irācarācēccurattip pōl mika apivirutti yaṭinta niliyil mika nerukkamuṭiyatākat tulaṅkukiṟatu.
ṭiyaṅmēṭu pala cilikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭākap peṟ ṟirukkiṟatu. cāṇṭi cirikāṇṭiyiṉ pirivil uḷḷa viṣṇu (tirumāl), nāṉmukaṉ civaṉ mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷ ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu kaḷākum. ataṉ ciṟpavēlippāṭukaḷ nuṭpam vāyntu api viruttiyaṭintuḷḷaṉa. cāṇṭi pīmāviṉ māṭattiluḷḷa cittiyap palakaṇiyiṉ ucci amippu mikak kavarcci taruvaṉavākum. itu ciṟpiyiṉ conta muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉāka eḻuntatākum. itaṉ tali, cāvakakkaliyiṉ purātaṉa muṟiyit taḻuviyatāka iruntālum alaṅkāra vēlippāṭukaḷum ciṉṉaṅkaḷum muṟṟilum porutta māka illi eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka uḷḷaṉa.
ṭiyaṅmēṭṭiṉ kiḻakkilum teṟkilum eṇṇaṟṟa ciṟu kōyilkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷ ellām aṭippaṭi yil itē muṟiyil amikkappaṭṭavikaḷākum. āṉāl caṟṟut tārāḷamākavum aṭikkaṭi vicittiraṅkaḷ kāṭṭum muṟiyilum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. campiṅmali arukil uḷḷa cāṇṭi cikiriyum ki.pi. 850 ām āṇṭil arumpiya civa cāṇṭipiriṅ kāppa eṉpatum eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mika mukkiyamāṉa kōyil keṭāṅ caṅkā eṉappaṭum, uṅkāraṉiṉ kōyilkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷākum. avikaḷ oṉpatu ciṟu kōyilkaḷiṉ tokutikaḷāka aṭaṅki mali ucciyil amintuḷḷaṉa. avi nalla puṉita yāttiri mārkkattil irukkiṉṟaṉa.
ippoḻutu ṭiyaṅ kōyilkaḷ appoḻutuḷḷa peyar  kaḷil vaḻaṅkap peṟātu cāṇṭi pīmā, cāṇṭi arccuṉā eṉṟa peyarkaḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuvaruvatu nāṭōṭi makkaḷiṉ katiyi aṉucarittup pārkkil atu mikavum kavarcciyākavē irukkum. āṉāl ataṉ varalāṟṟiyum mūla uṟpattiyiyum  uṇarvataṟku evvitat tuṇiyum utaviyum taramuṭiyātu. aṅkuḷḷa pala paṭimaṅ kaḷum civa camayappaṭimaṅkaḷākavē terikiṉṟaṉa. avi oru kālattil civaperumāṉi vaṇaṅkuvataṟkāka arppaṇikkappaṭ ṭirukka vēṇṭum. ittīvakattil ikkali putta camayattōṭu arukarukē intu camayamuṟiyum pirapalamāyc cāvaka varalāṟṟil intu cakāptam muḻuvatilum vaḷarntu vantuḷḷatu. nāḷāvaṭṭat til iru camaya neṟikaḷum viyakkattakka vitamākak kūṭi iruntiruk kiṉṟaṉa. cāvakattil civaperumāṉ palvēṟu muṟiyil vaṇaṅkap paṭṭālum pattirakuru eṉṟa peyaril peritum pōṟṟappaṭṭār. avar yōkiyāy, tāṭiyum, tacippaṟṟumuṭiyavarāyk kiyil kamaṇṭalamum jepamāliyum uṭiyavarāyk kāṇappaṭṭār.1
mattiya cāvakattil cumattirarkaḷiṉ āṭciyil (ki.pi. 732 mutal 860) - aḵtāvatu cilēntira marapiṉar āṭciyil arumpiya pala mukkiya niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷi nām ippoḻutu ālōcikka vēṇṭum. 778ām āṇṭil eḻunta cāṇṭikkalacāṉ cāvaka maṇṇil tali tūkkiya putta niṉivuc ciṉṉamāṉatu mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya vilimatikka voṇṇāta mukkiyamāṉa nikaḻcciyākum. aṅkuk kiṭitta cācaṉam mūlam cilēntira maṉṉaṉ arppaṇitta tārā tēviyiṉ tiruuruvam mattiya maṇṭa pattil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm intak kōyil perampāṉaṉ kōyilukku mēṟkup pakkattil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu. inta mēṭu atika nerukkamāṉa makkaḷ vāḻum nilapparappāka iruntatu. oru mukkiyamāṉa talinakarattiṉ pakutiyāka allatu mattiya cāvaka āṭcik kālam muḻuvatum talinakaramāka iruntatu. ṭiyaṅ mātiriyil irukkum cāṇṭik kalacāṉ putuppippataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum taṉi vāyil uḷḷa pakkak kōyilkaḷāka apivirutti aṭintu arukē puṭittirukkum māṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruntatu. piramāṇṭamāṉa kīrttimukā eṉṉum kalāmakarat tōraṇam piratāṉa vāyiliyum māṭaṅkaḷiyum aṇi ceytatu. makaratōraṇa vaḷivukaḷ kīḻē muḻuvatum vicittiramāṉa ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷ niṟintaṉavāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. cuvarkaḷ cuvimikka paṭṭikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa.

10. aṉṉiyar āṭciyil āyvu
iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākac (ki.pi. 1619 mutal 1798 vari) cāvakam ṭaccu, kiḻakkintiyak kumpiṉi āṭciyil iruntu vantatu. inta aṉṉiya āṭciyāḷarkaḷ iṅku vaṇikak kaṇṇōṭṭat tōṭu pukuntu piṉṉarc cāvaka nāṭṭik kavarntu ati aṭimip paṭutti āṇṭu vantaṉar. anta nāṭṭi iyaṉṟa aḷavu curaṇṭik koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟālum inta vaṇika maṉappāṉmi koṇṭa veḷḷiyarkaḷ aṅkup paravik kiṭanta nākarikattiyum niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷiyum kōyilkaḷiyum aḻipāṭukaḷiyum mikka āccariyattōṭu pārttu vantaṉar. patiṉeṭṭāvatu nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutivari avarkaḷiṭam oru vitapparivu uṇarcciyō allatu oru teḷivāṉa viḷakkamō arumpavilli. āṉāl oru mēleḻunta vāriyāṉa āpūrvamāṉa kaṇṇōṭṭam apivirutti aṭintu kalviyuṇarcciyāka eḻuntu āḻnta ārāycciyil iṟaṅkat tūṇṭiyatu. 1778 ām āṇṭu paṭṭō viyā viññāṉak kalikkaḻakam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉuṭiya nōkkam vicayaṅkaḷi ārāyvatu maṭṭumaṉṟu, vivacāyam, vaṇikam intak kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭṭiṉ celvac ceḻippil akkari celuttuvatu mākum. āṉāl, atōṭu iyaṟki varalāṟu, paṇṭikkāla niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ, makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ ākiyavikaḷiyum āytalākum.
ivikaḷip paṟṟi mutaṉmutalāka akkari koṇṭu cāvakattiṉ paḻamperum ciṉṉaṅkaḷi āya muṉ vantavarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavar aṅku 1801 ām āṇṭu mutal 1808 ām āṇṭu vari vaṭakiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkarik kavarṉarāka irunta nikkōla eṅkalhārṭ eṉpavarākum. avaratu tūṇṭutaliṉ mītu peram pāṉaṉ aḻipāṭukaḷum iṉṉum pala kōyilkaḷum cuttappaṭṭup paricōtikkappaṭṭaṉa. ṭaccu kavarṉar intap paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ ārāycci ārvattāl atikārappūrmākap pala arumperum paṭimaṅkaḷiyellām kōyiliṉiṉṟu avaratu cemrāṅ (Semarang) eṉṉum iṭattil nāṭṭuppuṟa illamākiya ṭi varuca hiṭ eṉṉum iṭattiṟkuk koṇṭu pōka uttaraviṭṭār. appāl ivikaḷellām ulāntā nāṭṭiluḷḷa leyṭaṉ nakariluḷḷa maṉita iṉap parappup paṟṟiya āyviyal (Ethnography) paḻam poruḷ kāṭciccāliyil iṭampeṟṟaṉa.
appāl piriṭṭiṣ āṭciyiṉ celvākkuc cāvakattil vaḷarntiruntapoḻutu (1811 - 16) ciṟanta aṟivāḷiyāyum, aracap piratinitiyāyum (Governor - General) āka viḷaṅkiyavar car ṭāmpōrṭu rēval (Sir. Stamford Raffles) eṉpavar āvar. avar varalāṟṟu ārāycci, putiporuḷ ārāycci ākiya iruperun tuṟikaḷi lum akkālattirunta evariyum viṭac ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉār. avaratu ātaravil cāvakattil paḻam poruḷ ārāycci toṭarntu periya aḷavil naṭipeṟṟu vantatu. ataṉ viḷivākac cāvakattiṉ varalāṟu (laṇṭaṉ 1817) eṉṟa arumperum nūl veḷivantatu. intac ciṟanta nūl veḷivantataṉ mūlam rēvalkku mikunta kuravam vantatu. cāvakap putiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa iṉṟiya māṇavarkaḷukkumkūṭa avaratu nūl mikavum payaṉuḷḷatāka irukkiṟatu. āṉāl varalāṟṟup pakutiyil perumpālāṉavi nampakkūṭiya takavalkaḷ alla - avikaḷ nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷiṉ kuṟippukaḷiṉiṉṟum cācaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikippaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭa vācakaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum eḻutappaṭṭavi kaḷākum. eṉavē avikaḷi mika eccarikkiyōṭu eṭuttāḷa vēṇṭum. avarukkup piṉ vanta āciriyar jēm perkūcaṉ (James Fergusson) eṉpavar āvar. avar rēval nūli nampi, varalāṟu campantamāṉa takavalkaḷi meyyeṉa nampit tam nūlil pala iṭaṅ kaḷil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭip perun tavaṟukku āṭpaṭṭār.
paḻamperuñ cāvakattiṉ niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷiyum kōyil kaḷiyum ārāya virumpip pala aṟiñarkaḷ tamatu vilimatikka oṇṇā kālattiyum caktiyiyum kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil celaviṭṭuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ palar ṭaccu nāṭṭic cērntavarka ḷāvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ annāṭṭiṉ palkalik kaḻakattil payiṉṟu uyarnta kalippaṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟavarkaḷāvārkaḷ. avarkaḷil nāṉ iṅkuk kuṟippiṭattakkavar pērāciriyar heṇṭirik kerṉ (Hendrik Kern) avarukku vaṭamoḻiyilum  cāvaka moḻiyilum nalla pulamiyuṇṭu. cāvakattiṉ paṇṭikkāla nūlkaḷ eḻutap peṟṟirunta kāvi moḻiyiyum avar kaṟṟiruntār. cāvakattil vaṭamoḻiyilum, kāvi moḻiyilum variyappaṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷi viyakkattakkavāṟu avar patippittuḷḷār. vēṟu pala aṟivāḷi kaḷiṉ nūṟkaḷōṭu aṟiñar kerṉ eḻutiya paḻiya cāvaka irāmā yaṇamum patippikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉoṭu ataṉ ṭaccu moḻi peyarppum veḷi vantuḷḷatu.
aṟiñar kerṉ eṉpavariṉ tiṟami mikka māṇavar ṭākṭar pirāṇṭa eṉpavarākum. avar intōṉēciya āṭcipparappil vāḻpavar. kalittuṟiyilum putiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyi lum naṉku payiṟci peṟṟavar. paḻiya moḻikaḷilum navīṉa moḻi kaḷilum ariya aṟivuttiṟam peṟṟavar. cāvaka niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷi ārāyvataṟkākap paṭṭēviya aracāṅkattāl niyamaṉañ ceyyappeṟṟavar. avar eḻutiya iraṇṭu taṉipperum nūlkaḷ - aḵtāvatu oṉṟu jākōviṉ kōyilip paṟṟiyatu (1904). maṟṟatu avar iṟanta piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷākiya ciṅkācāriyip paṟṟi yum paṉatāraṉip paṟṟiyatumākum (1909). irumātiri nūlkaḷum aḻakiya paṭaṅkaḷōṭu iyaṉṟa aḷavu muyaṉṟu niṟivuṭiya nūlāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
1905 ām āṇṭu aṟiñar pirāṇṭa avarkaḷiṉ akāla maraṇattiṟkup piṉ aintu āṇṭukaḷ uruṇṭōṭiṉa. appāl avaratu iṭattil ṭākṭar. eṉ. jē. kurōm niyamikkap peṟṟār. avar oru putiporuḷ ārāyccik kuḻuvi nirmāṇittu ārāyntu vantār.
appērārāycciyiṉ payaṉākac cāvakattiṉ paḻiya kali, varalāṟu, kalveṭṭu ākiyavikaḷip paṟṟip pala arum perum ārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ veḷivantaṉa. appāl avar 1915 ām āṇṭil ōyvu peṟṟa piṉṉar intu cāvakakkaliyiṉ kinūl1 (Hand Book of Hindu Javaness Art) eṉṟa ōr uyarnta nūli eḻuti iraṇṭu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭār.
annūlkaḷ niṟiyat tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa takavalkaḷiyum paṭaṅkaḷiyum uṭiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. pōrōputūriṉ tali ciṟanta niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷāl cāvakattiṉ putiporuḷkaḷi viḷakkum mākṉam āpa (Magnum opus); 444 periya pakkaṅkaḷik koṇṭa uyarnta vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa tāḷkaḷil mikac ciṟanta aḻakiya niḻaṟpaṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa ciṟanta aḻakiya nūlāka veḷivantatu.2
1919 ām āṇṭu ṭicampar mātam ṭākṭar kurōm leyṭaṉ palkalik kaḻakattil cāvakap putiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟik kāka ārampikkappaṭṭa putiya iṭattiṟkup pērāciriyarāy niyamaṉañ ceyyappaṭṭār. avarukkup piṉ anta iṭattiṟku vantavar cāvakattiṉ puti poruḷ ārāyccittuṟit talivarāy irunta ṭākṭar ve.ṭi.kē. pōc eṉpavarāvār. avarukku oru ciṟiya utavi nipuṇarum iruntu vantār. ippoḻutu avarē paḻam poruḷkaḷip pātukāttal ārāytal eṉṉum iruperum paṇikaḷiyum naṭatti varukiṉṟār. avaratu ciṟanta paṇi atikāri kaḷukkum aracāṅkattiṟkum perum matippi aḷittuḷḷatu. itaṉ mūlam cāvakattil eḻunta intu nākarikattiṉ ciṟappu ulāntāvil maṭṭumiṉṟi airōppā muḻuvatilum paraviyatu.
cāvakattil uḷḷa makkaḷum iṉṟuvari viḻippuṟṟuc cāvakattil pīṭuṟa niṉṟa kalik karuvūlatti ārāyamuṉvaravilli. āṉāl cumār nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ vaṅkāḷattil māperum intiyakkaḻakam (Greater India Society) eṉṟa oru niṟuvaṉam kaṇṭu ciṟitu kālam cāvakak kalikaḷippaṟṟi ārāycci ceyya muyaṉṟaṉar. anta uṇarcci nīṇṭanāḷ nilittu niṟkavilli. iṉṟu cāvaka aracāṅkam mulīm aracāṅkamāy iruppatāl intuk kōyil kaḷiyum, paṭimaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟi ārāyvataṟku akkari koḷḷa villi. intiya aracāṅkam matac cārpaṟṟa aracāṅkamāy iruppa tāl cāvakattil nilaviya intuk kalikaḷippaṟṟi ārāyamuṟpaṭu vatu aracāṅkattiṉ mūlakkoḷkikku māṟāka irukkum eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
piriṭṭaṉ, pirañcu aracāṅkaṅkaḷ kiṟitava aracāṅkaṅ kaḷāka iruntālum cāvakattiluḷḷa intiyak kalikaḷil akkari celuttiyatu kaṇṭu nām peritum pārāṭṭa vēṇṭiyavarāy irukkiṉṟōm. iṅkilāntu pōṉṟa nāṭṭiliruntu avarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭāka irunta intiyā, ilaṅki, malēciyā, ciṅkappūr, intōṉēciyā, intō cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku vanta puti poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉar maṭṭumiṉṟi āṭci ceyta kōṭṭattalivar āḷunar pōṉṟavarkaḷum taṅkaḷiṉ aracāṅkap paṇiyōṭamiyātu, itara kalit tuṟikaḷilum akkari celuttip perumpaṇi ceytu vantiruppatu nammāl eṉṟum maṟakkaṟpāla taṉṟu. iṉṟiya cutantira intiyāviṉ piratinitikaḷāyum tūtuvar kaḷāyum, vaṇikak kuḻuviṉarkaḷāyum, kāriyataricikaḷāyum iruppa varkaḷ anta nāṭṭil uḷḷa intiyak kalikaḷiyum nākarikattip paṟṟiyum cintikka vēṇṭum.
iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ki.pi. 1746il mattiya cāvakattil uḷḷa perampāṉaṉil aracappirati nitiyāka (kavarṉar ceṉaralāka) iruntavar vāṉ imhōv eṉpavar ākum. avar perampāṉaṉ pakutiyil uḷḷa tolporuḷkaḷik kaṇṭukaḷikka virumpi aṅkuc ceṉṟatākak cāvakattippaṟṟi mutaṉmutalāka eḻutiya āciriyar oruvar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. aṅku aracappiratiniti kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟil turkkiyiṉ veṇkalap paṭimamum oṉṟu. atik kaṇṭatum avarukku aṭiyil varum eṇṇam eḻuntatu. atu, cāvakarkaḷ muṉṉar yūtarkaḷō, kiṟitavarkaḷō, ilāmiyarkaḷō, illāta puṟac camayattārkaḷiṭiyē yuḷḷa pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉarāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatēyākum. āṅkilēya atikārikaḷ 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampattilum, intiyāvil cēvi ceytapiṉ cāvakattiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭa piṉṉarum intiyāvilum cāvakattilumuḷḷa niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷip paṟṟi ārāya muṟpaṭṭaṉar eṉpatu kuṟittu āccariyap paṭuvataṟkilli. intiyāviṟkum cāvakattiṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpu kaḷiyum vēṟupala ceytikaḷiyum paṟṟi avarkaḷ nuṇuki ārāyn tuḷḷaṉar. car. ulliyam jōṉ, mārṭaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ intōṉēciya moḻikaḷiluḷḷa vaṭamoḻip pataṅkaḷippaṟṟi yellām ārāyntu vantaṉar. uylhelm vāṉ hampōlṭ (Wilhelm Von Humbolt) eṉpavar eḻutiya periya nūlākiya upar ṭi kāvi - pirāc (Ulber die Kauvi - Sprache) eṉpatu avar iṟantapiṉ ki.pi. 1836 ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.1 atil, cāvakattiṉ varalāṟu campantappaṭṭa ātāraṅkaḷi ārāya muṟpaṭṭāl eṇṇaṟṟa cācaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam intiyarkaḷiṉ celvākku avar kaḷiṉ moḻi, eḻuttu, kali, camayaṅkaḷ mūlam paravi iruntaṉa eṉpatit teḷivāka aṟiya muṭiyum. cāvakattiṉ varalāṟu campanta māka eḻutappeṟṟa iru nūlkaḷ cāvakattil uḷḷaṉa. avi paḻiya cāvaka moḻi allatu kāvi moḻiyil uḷ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu putiporuḷ ārāycci mutaliyavikaḷippaṟṟi variyap paṭṭaṉavākum. avikaḷil oṉṟāṉa nākarakirēṭṭakāmā (Nagarakratagam) eṉpatu 1894 ām āṇṭu lōmpōk paṭi yeḻucciyiṉ pōtu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatu. aḵtu iṟutiyāka irunta pālit tīviṉ talivaṉ tī mūṭṭiya poḻutu intak ki yeḻuttup pirati ṭākṭar pirāṇṭa eṉpavarāl kāppāṟṟap paṭṭatu. inta nūl eppaṭi iruppiṉum atu ki.pi. 1365 ām āṇṭu niṟivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. irunūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākak kiḻakkuc cāvakattil āṭcipurinta marapiṉarkaḷip paṟṟiyatu. itil intiyā viṟkum cāvakattiṟkumuḷḷa paṇṭikkālat toṭarpukaḷ eviyum kāṇappaṭavilli.
cīṉanāṭṭu vaḻippōkkaṉ varinta payaṇak kuṟippukkaḷilum cīṉa aracāṅkattiṉ kālakkiramamāṉa varalāṟṟilum kāṇak kiṭakkum takavalkaḷik koṇṭum niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum cila cāṉṟukaḷik koṇṭum  intiya - cāvakat toṭarpukaḷi oruvāṟu aṟiyalām. paḻaṅkālattil vanta paricutta payaṇikaḷil pāhiyaṉ (Fa Hien) eṉpavar ilaṅkiyiṉiṉṟu cīṉam pōntāṉ. puyalāl avaṉatu kappal tākkappaṭṭu allalkaḷukku āḷāki (avaṉ kuṟippiṭum) ī - pō - ti (Ye - Po - Ti) eṉṉum iṭatti aṭintāṉ. atu cāvakam allatu cumantirāvāka irukka vēṇṭum.
avar, inta nāṭṭil ēṟpamiti illāta kōṭpāṭṭil paṟṟuṭiyavarkaḷum, purōkita matattil nampikkiyuṭiyavar kaḷum niṟintirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl putta pakavāṉiṉ vitikaḷ avarkaḷukku atikam teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ivar tarum takaval mūlam ki.pi. 400 ām āṇṭil iṅkuc civacamaya nākarikam paravi niṉṟatu. putta matam peritum kāṇappaṭavilli eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ivar civa neṟiyi ataṉ caṭaṅkācāraṅkaḷik koṇṭu purōkita matam eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ivar tarum takaval varalāṟu campantappaṭṭa ōr uṟutiyāṉa ceytiyākum.
appāl ki.pi. 671 ām āṇṭu maṟṟōr cīṉa yāttirik kārar iṭciṅ (I - tsing) intiyāviṟkuc cellum poḻutu ḵpō - ccē (Fo - che) eṉṟa iṭattik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. atu pālampāṅ eṉṟa cumattirāt tīvil uḷḷa iṭamākum. aṅku appoḻutu puttamatam naṉṟākap paravi iruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ḵpō - ccē intu nākarikattiṉ miya niliyamākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. eṉiṉum aṅku avar vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam payilum poruṭṭu āṟu mātam taṅkiyatāka avaruṭiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mūlam terikiṟatu.
pirañcup pērāciriyar ṭākṭar jiyārja kōyiṭṭa mēṟkuc cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟil ki.pi. 500 ām āṇṭu mutal 1300ām āṇṭu vari putintu kiṭanta  arumperum nikaḻccikaḷi yellām ārāyntu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār. ivaratu ārāycci veḷivarumuṉ mēṟkuc cāvakattip paṟṟi oru ciṟitutāṉ ulakattiṟkut teriyum. malāyt tīpakaṟpattiṉ mēṟkup pakutiyil valimi mikka intu malāy kappaṟpaṭi veṟṟimuracu koṭṭi niṉṟatu. inta āṭciyē srī vijaya āṭci eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. atu cilēntira araca marapiṉarkku uriyatāka iruntatu. intat tīpakaṟpat tiṉ varalāṟu vēṟu enta varalāṟṟiyum viṭak kavarccimikkatāyum niṉittāl perum uḷḷakkiḷarcciyi ūṭṭuvatākavum uḷḷatu. aṟiñar kōyiṭṭa ārāycci veḷivantu ulakiluḷḷa pala aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷatti īrttatu. eṉavē avaratu ārāycciyi uṟutippaṭuttavum avarāl viṭappaṭṭa pala nikaḻccikaḷi mēṟ koṇṭu ārāyavum ataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷit tēṭavum, muṉṉark kaṇṭa pala ceytikaḷōṭu putitāyk kaṇṭa uṇmikaḷi iṇittum cāvaka varalāṟṟi oru niṟivāṉa varalāṟākat tikaḻum paṭi ceyya muyaṟci eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kōyiṭṭa1 avarkaḷit tavira kurōyeṉvelṭ (Groeneveldt)1 pelliyaṭ (Pelliot)2 perrāṇṭ (Ferrand)3 kurōm (Krom)4 ṭaṭṭā. heym (Stutterheim)5, macumtār (Mazumdar) II,6 piricilaki (Przyluski)7 pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ cāvaka varalāṟṟi aṇuki ārāyntu intak kālakaṭṭa varalāṟṟiṟkup putiya veḷiccatti aḷittaṉar. iḵtaṉṟi itaṟkup piṉ aṇmik kālattil pala aṟiñarkaḷ cāvaka varalāṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭum ārāyntu ati apivirutti ceyya muṉvantaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi muṉkūṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalōṭu iṇittuk koḷvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. avarkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavar jē.el. mōyaṉ eṉpavarākum. avar ittīpakaṟpattiṉ varalāṟṟil mutal āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ virivāṉa maṟumalarcciyi ārāya muyaṉṟuḷḷār. ivar cāvaka nāṭṭuc ceytikaḷippaṟṟi ārāynta varalāṟṟu māṇavarkaḷil ciṟappumikkavarākum. avar kiyāṇṭa vaḻikaḷāl tikipput tarum mukkiyamāṉa ceytikaḷ atikam illāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. avarukku muṉ ārāynta aṟiñar kaḷip pōlaṉṟi mikak kaṇṭippāṉa uḷḷattiṉ vivēkamuḷḷa muṟiyip peṟṟiruntār. intap pakutikaḷil payaṇañ ceyta paṇṭikkāla vaḻippōkkarkaḷāṉa arēpiyarkaḷ cīṉarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷiṉ kuṟippukkaḷiliruntu tokukkap peṟṟa nila iyal paṟṟiya ceytikaḷiṉ tokuti aṟiñar mōyaṉsiṉ vātattiṟku mukkiyamāṉa aṭippaṭiyāyiruntatu.
cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟippaṟṟiyum makkaḷiṉ nampikkikaḷ, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, kalikaḷiṉ maṟumalarcci ākiyavikaḷip paṟṟiyum tiru. mōyaṉ avarkaḷiṉ tiṟamimikka ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāy uṇmiyāṉa nili ulakiṟku aṟivikkappaṭṭatu.
avaratu ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka intu - malēya āṭci malēciyat tīpakaṟpattil niṟuvappaṭṭiruntatu eṉṟu uṟutip paṭṭatu. tamiḻakattil naṭipeṟṟa pōrkaḷiṉ payaṉāka iṅkup pala nikaḻccikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. veṟṟi peṟṟavarkaḷ malēyat tīpakaṟpattil pala akatikaḷik kuṭiyēṟṟiṉar. intak  kōṭpāṭṭiṉ payaṉāka ki.pi. 300 ām āṇṭil mūlavarmaṉ marapiṉar āṭciyiṉ kīḻkuṭē āṭci eḻuntatu. itu pallava maṉṉaṉāl iksavāku nāṭṭi viṭṭu viraṭṭappaṭṭatiṉ payaṉākum. itē karuttuppaṭi mēṟkuc cāvakattil tarumaṉ irājyam vīḻntatu. intiya cakkaravartti camuttirakuptaṉāl cālaṅkāyaṉa maṉṉaṉ tōṟkaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.  ki.pi. 518 āṇṭukku muṉ pālampāṅ irācciyam (paḻiya maliyu pōli allatu mā - li) tiṭumeṉat toṭarpaṟṟatu. kuṇṭiṉiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ kīḻ viṣṇukuṇṭiṉ eṉṟa āntira aracaṉāl viraṭṭappaṭṭu nāṭukaṭattappaṭṭa (putta) kāntāri mattiya cāvakattil 609 āṇṭukaḷukkuppiṉ 616 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ pōli allatu pāṭi eṉṟa irācciyatti nilināṭṭiṉāṉ. itaṉ palaṉākak kaliṅkar āṭciyiṉar cāvakattik kippaṟṟiṉar. kaṅkā - kaliṅkar āṭci celuttiṉāṉ. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari intiyāviluḷḷa kaliṅkattiliruntu vanta cañcayaṉiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ malakkāvil kaliṅka āṭcikku aṭikōliṉar eṉṟu kaliṅkāviṉ, cīṉa nakal piratikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
724 - 8ām āṇṭil keṭā (kaṭāram) vīḻcci aṭintatu. ataṉ palaṉāka akatikaḷ eṅkum malintaṉar. cañcayaṉ cilēntira rōṭu kūṭi akatikaḷukku mattiya cāvakattil nalla uṟiviṭam kaṇ ṭāṉ. itaṉmūlam cilēntira maṉṉarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku muṉpōnta paṉaṉkāraṉ cāṇṭikkalacāṉik kaṭṭiyatupōl 778ām āṇṭil cāṇṭimeṇṭ eṉṟa niṉivuc ciṉṉattik kaṭṭi muṭittaṉar.
caṉ - pō - ṭsiyiṉ āṭci cōḻarōṭu pōrāṭi 1023 - 24; 1030 ām āṇṭukaḷil taṟkālikamākat tōlviyuṟṟu mīṇṭum tali tūkkiyatu. atupōl erlaṅkā mīṇṭum talitūkkavum cilēntirar āṭciyi akaṟṟic cāvakattil amitiyiyum oṟṟumiyiyum nilināṭṭiṉāṉ.
ivikaḷiyellām mōyaṉ tam ārāycciyil mika virivāy ārāyntu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.1
ivvāṟu ulāntā, pirañcu, iṅkilāntu mutaliya nāṭṭi luḷḷa pēraṟiñarkaḷ palar cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟi naṉku ārāyntu pala kaṭṭurikaḷiyum nūlkaḷiyum veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. mēlum inta nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa putiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉic cērnta aṟiñarkaḷ nāṭu muḻuvatum kāṭu malikaḷi yum tēṭittirintum pūmiyi akaḻntu ārāyntum kiṭitta palvēṟu kaṭṭiṭap pakutikaḷiyum cilikaḷiyum aṇikaḷiyum pāttiraṅkaḷiyum āyutaṅkaḷiyum taṅkaḷ putiporuḷkaḷ cēkarittu vittirukkum kaṇkāṭcic cāliyil vittu ārāyntu pala nūlkaḷiyum tuṇṭup piracuraṅkaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
inta ārāyccit tuṟiyil paṅku koṇṭavarkaḷ peritum ayal nāṭṭārkaḷāy irukkiṉṟārkaḷē yoḻiya intiyar kaḷō, tamiḻakarkaḷō, intōṉēciyarkaḷō atikamākak kāṇap paṭāmal iruppatu nām cintikka vēṇṭiya toṉṟākum.

11. cāvakap pāvikkūttu
tamiḻakattil cumār 50 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vari intap pāvikkūttukkaḷ eṅkum mikap paravalāka naṭipeṟṟu vantaṉa. kirāma makkaḷ iravellām viḻittiruntu intap pāvikkūttip pārttu makiḻntu vantaṉar. intap pāvik kūttu niḻaṟpaṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum pommikaḷi vittu āṭṭuvatip pomma lāṭṭam eṉṟum pukalvar. intap pāvikkūttu terukkūttu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. oru periya teruviṉ ōrattil oru ciṟiya koṭṭaki pōṭappaṭṭirukkum. muṉpakkam oru veḷḷit tuṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. uḷḷē oru oḷimikka viḷakku eriyum. kūtti naṭattupavar tayār ceyyappaṭṭirukkum katikkuriya paṭaṅkaḷik kiyil vittuk koṇṭu ataṉ niḻal tuṇiyil piratipalikkumpaṭi ceyvar. paṭaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻē nīṇṭirukkum kuccukaḷip piṭittuk koṇṭu paṭaṅkaḷi iyakkuvar. avarkaḷ kikaḷō avarkaḷ iruntu iyakkuvatō, pārkkum makkaḷukkut teriyātavāṟu aṭippakkam karupputtuṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. intak kūttarkaḷ kikaḷil palapaṭaṅkaḷi orē camayattil vittu iyakkuvar. paṭaṅkaḷiṉ kikaḷum, kālkaḷum ēṉ? talikaḷ kūṭa iyaṅkum. paṭaṅkaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉumākak koṇṭupōkap paṭuvatu mūlam peritākavum ciṟitākavum kāṇappaṭum. pōrkaḷ, tirumaṇaṅkaḷ mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷ ellām atikac ciṟappuṭaṉ kāṭṭappaṭum. iḵtu iṉṟiya ciṉimāp paṭam pōṉṟatāka irukkum. ciṉimā vantu intiyāvil intak kaliyi aṭiyōṭu aḻittuviṭṭatu.
cāvakattil pāvikkūttum pommalāṭṭamum iṉṟum ciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. intak kūttaraṅkaṅkaḷ palavita amippukkaḷi uṭiyaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. mikat toṉmi yāṉavi vāyaṅpepar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. niḻalāṭṭatti vāyaṅ purvar eṉṟum pommalāṭṭatti vāyaṅkeṭāk vāyaṅ kiḷiṭṭik eṉṟum kūṟuvar. intak katikaḷ perumpālum  cāvaka varalāṟṟit taḻuvi intiya itikācaṅkaḷāṉa irāmāyaṇam pāratamum mutaliyavikaḷōṭu ārampamāki macāpākit maṉṉar kaḷōṭu muṭivaṭiyum. intap pāvikkūttu cīṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭatā allatu intiya nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭu collappaṭṭa kaliyā eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl aṟuti yiṭṭu urikka muṭiyavilli. āṉāl nammip poṟuttavariyil tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka intap pāvikkūttu intiya mūlatti uṭiya teṉṟu karutukiṟōm. pommalāṭṭam pommikaḷik kayiṟ ṟāl kaṭṭi mēliruntu iḻuppatumūlam pommikaḷi iyakku vatākum.
iṉṟu ulakameṅkum makkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷik kavarntu niṟpatu oḷi uruvappaṭaṅkaḷēyākum (ciṉimāvēyākum). muṟkālattil vāḻnta tamiḻmakkaḷ intap paṭakkāṭcikkup patilāka nāṭakaṅ kaḷiyē naṭatti vantaṉar. avikaḷik kūttu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. nāṭakattil āṭalum pāṭalum iṇintiruntaṉa. nāṭaka vaḷarcci yil tuṇiyākap pala icik karuvikaḷ iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭaka araṅkukaḷ nākarika muṟiyil eḻuntuḷḷaṉa. nāṭakatti niṟi vuṭiya tākkap palavaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭiya taṭṭikaḷum tiriccīli kaḷum toṅkaviṭappaṭṭaṉa. tiriccīlikaḷip paḻantamiḻar eḻiṉi eṉṟaṉar. inta eḻiṉi, orumuka eḻiṉi, porumuka eḻiṉi, karantuvaral eḻiṉi eṉṟu mūṉṟa vakippaṭṭatāy iruntatu. intat tirikaḷil orupakkam orumuka eḻiṉiyum, maṟṟoru pakkam porumuka eḻiṉiyum, mēlē karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum irukkum. orumuka eḻiṉi eṉpatu orupakkamiruntu maṟṟoru pakkam vari taḷḷakkūṭiya tiri. porumuka eḻiṉi eṉpatu iraṇṭākap pirikkappaṭṭu iru pakkaṅkaḷiliruntum taḷḷakkūṭiya tiri. karantuvaral eḻiṉi mēlē maṟivāyc cuṟṟivikkappaṭṭirukkum tiri. itaṉic curuḷ cañciki eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu. araṅkiṉ uḷḷē aṇi aṇiyāy aḻakiya nili viḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟappaṭṭirukkum. avikaḷ mikunta nuṇṇaṟivuṭaṉ viḷakkiṉ niḻal āṭupavarkaḷ mītō kāṭciyik kāṇavantiruppavarkaḷ mītō viḻātapaṭi takka iṭattil vikkappaṭṭirukkum.
ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭuuṭaṉ poliyat
tōṟṟiya araṅkil toḻutaṉar ēttap
pūtari eḻuti mēlnili vittut
tūṇ niḻal puṟappaṭa māṇviḷak keṭuttāṅku
orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyum
karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttāṅku
ōviya vitāṉattu uripeṟu nittilattu
mālit tāmam vaḷiyuṭaṉ nāṟṟi
viruntupaṭak kiṭanta varuntoḻil araṅkattu					(cilap. araṅ. 105-113)
eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟukiṟatu. eṉavē mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻakattil nāṭakakkali ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyiruntatu eṉpataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. antap paḻaṅkālattilē viḷakkiṉ veḷiccattil uruvaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvatiyum peri tāvatiyum aṟintiruntaṉar. atōṭu nāṭakattil varum makkaḷ katāpāttiraṅkaḷit tavira āṭumāṭu, yāṉi, puli, ciṅkam, kuraṅku, kaḻuku, mayil, puṟā, karuṭaṉ, pāmpu pōṉṟavikaḷip piṭittu araṅkattilē koṇṭu varamuṭiyāmal iruntatāl palakikaḷil allatu tōlkaḷil vilaṅkukaḷ allatu paṟavikaḷ uruvaṅ kaḷit tīṭṭi vaṇṇamūṭṭi ati iyakki vantuḷḷaṉar.
nāṭakam maṉṉarkaḷiṉ ātaravu peṟṟa ōr uyarnta kaliyāka miḷirntatu. kōyilkaḷil nāṭaka araṅkukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu atil periya aṟiñarkaḷ nāṭakaṅkaḷil naṭittu vantaṉar. maṉṉarkaḷ atik kaṇṭu cuvittu naṭikarkaḷukkup pala paricukaḷ aḷittu vantaṉar.
āṉāl pāvikkūttu (niḻalāṭṭam), pommalāṭṭam eṉṉum kalikaḷit tamiḻakattiṉar attuṇi uyarnta kaliyāka matikkavilli. pāmara makkaḷiṉ kaliyākavum, ciṟṟūrkaḷil ēḻimakkaḷ cuvikkum kaliyākavum iruntatu. itu ciṟṟūrkaḷil terukkōṭiyil celaviṉṟi oru ciṟu mēṭi kampukaḷāl amittuc cuṟṟilum mūṉṟu puṟamum mēṟpuṟamum teṉṉaṅ kīṟṟiṉāl maṟikkappaṭṭu muṉpakkam oru paḻiya veḷḷit tuṇiyik kaṭṭi uḷḷē viḷakkēṟṟi vittuk koṇṭu tōliṉālāṉa uruvaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam muṉpakkamuḷḷa tuṇiyil niḻalākak kāṭṭuvārkaḷ. itaṉvāyilākap peruṅkatikaḷum mikac culapamāka makkaḷukkuk kāṭṭappaṭum. makkaḷ viṭiyum vari ayarātu kaṇṭukaḷippar. itu makkaḷiṉ terukkūttukkaḷil oṉṟāka matikkappaṭṭu vantatu. aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vari viḷakka mākac colvatāṉāl ciṉimāk kāṭci talittūkkum vari ikkali tamiḻ nāṭeṅkum paravi iruntatu. ciṉimāk kāṭciyiṉ mūlam nāṭakak kaliyum pāvikkūttum pommalāṭṭamum kāṭēṟiviṭṭaṉa.
intap pāvikkūtti iyakkum cūttiratāriyiṉ tiṟami viyakkattakkatu. intac cūttiratāri kayiṟṟi iḻuttu iyakkuvataṉ mūlam kūttu muḻuvatum naṭi peṟukiṟatu. ivaṉatu iyakkun tiṟami pōṟṟattakkatu. intak kaliyil oru kuṭumpam muḻuvatumē īṭupaṭum. ici uriyāṭal palavitamāṉa olikaḷik kiḷappal mutaliyavikaḷukku oru kuṭumpattil uḷḷa kaṇavaṉ, maṉivi, makkaḷ, aṇṇaṉ, tampi mutaliya aṉivarum īṭupaṭṭu vantārkaḷ. kirāma makkaḷ ūril varivacūlittup pāvikkūttukkārarkaḷukku utavi vantaṉar. uṇavu uṭi mutaliya aṉpaḷippukaḷum aḷittu vantaṉar.
pāvikaḷ āṭṭuttōl, erumittōl, melliya palaki pōṉṟavaṟṟāl amittu avikaḷukkēṟṟa vaṇṇantīṭṭi āṭikaḷum aṇikaḷum poliyak kalittiṟamiyōṭu naṭatti vantaṉar.
āntira nāṭṭil uḷḷa nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷ pāvik kūttiṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷāka neṭuṅkālamāka iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. kumāracampavam, uttara harivamcam, iraṅkanāta irāmāyaṇam, pala nāṭṭiṉ vīracarittiram, campōpākkiyāṉam, pāñcālipariṇayam eṉṉum teluṅku nūlkaḷ itippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. intak kūttic civarāttiri viḻāvil ciricilam pōṉṟa kōyilkaḷil naṭatti ṉārkaḷ eṉṟum makāpāratam pōṉṟa kāviyaṅkaḷiliruntu eṭutta katikaḷē itaṉ karuvāka iruntaṉa eṉṟum pālkuṟiki cōmanātar iyaṟṟiya paṇṭitārātya carittiram terivikkiṟatu.
āntira nāṭṭilum karnāṭaka nāṭṭilum cūtrata kompē (niḻalāṭṭam) cakkalat kompē (pommalāṭṭam) eṉpavaṟṟiṉ nilikkaḷamāka yaṭcakāṉam eṉṟa ici nāṭakam upayōkap paṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. irunāṭukaḷilum yaṭcakāṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vaḷamuṭiyaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa.
uṇmiyil itaṟkākavē teluṅkuk kavikaḷ yaṭcakāṉaṅ kaḷi eḻutiṉārkaḷ. mariṅkaṇṭi paṭṭaru irāmanucāccāriyār (ki.pi. 1850) iyaṟṟiya ciri irāma nāṭakam eṉṟa yaṭcakāṉam pommalāṭṭa irāmāyaṇam eṉṟu pukaḻ aṭintirukkiṟatu.
āntira nāṭṭil neṭuṅkālamāka inta āṭṭam kuṭumpak kaliyāki viṭṭatu. irāyala cīmā neṭuṅkālamākap pāvikkūt tāṭum kuṭumpattiṉarukku nilikkaḷamāka iruntu varukiṟatu. attakiya kuṭumpattiṉar maṉṉarkaḷiṭam parici peṟṟārkaḷ; iṉām kirāmaṅkaḷi aṭintārkaḷ. irāyala cīmiyil pomma lāṭṭam eṉṟa peyarōṭu ōr ūrē irukkiṟatu. aṅkē pala kaviñarkaḷ iruntu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷil oruvarākiya pommalāṭṭa sāmpiyā eṉpavar (ki.pi. 1710) teluṅkil kirātārccu nīyam eṉṟa yaṭcakāṉattiyum kaṉṉaṭattil iraṇṭu yaṭcakāṉaṅkaḷiyum iyaṟṟiṉār.
āntira nāṭṭil uḷḷa pommalāṭṭakkārarkaḷ makārāṣṭira tēcattiṉiṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ kākatīyap pēraraciṉ ātaravil kaliyiṉpam vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. cilar teliṅkāṉāvilum cilar irāyala cīmiyilum āntira tēcattuk kaṭaṟkariyōrap pakutikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. cilar kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟkum cilar kēraḷa nāṭṭiṟkum ceṉṟārkaḷ. jāvā mutaliya tīvukaḷukkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷum uṇṭu. avarkaḷālētāṉ pomma lāṭṭam jāvāvil vaḷarntu varukiṟatu pōlum eṉṟa, karuttup paṭa ṭākṭar e.vi. jōkarāv avarkaḷ āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷa tirivēṇi pattirikiyil virivāka oru kaṭṭuri eḻutiyuḷḷār.1
iḵtaṉṟi nīṇṭakālamāka intiyāvil pāvikkūttu eṉṉum niḻalāṭṭam makkaḷukku makiḻcciyūṭṭak kūṭiyatākavum nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷiṉ kaliyākavum iruntu vantuḷḷatu eṉṟu ē.pi. kīt pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ataṉōṭu mika vaḷarcciyaṭintu nāṭeṅkum paravi niṉṟa nāṭakak kalikku niḻalāṭṭam aṭippaṭiyāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟum karutukiṟārkaḷ. kiṟitava ūḻi eḻuntu āyiram āṇṭiṟku appāl tāṉ intiyāvil piṟantu vaḷarnta inta niḻalāṭṭam eṉṉum kaliyē veḷināṭṭiṟkup pōyirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. cīṉanāṭṭilum ikkali paravi iruntatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. cāvakat tīviṟku intiyāviṉiṉṟē inta niḻalāṭṭak kali ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉac cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ. mukamūṭi aṇintu āṭum oru pēyāṭṭam cāvakattil uṇṭu. cīṉattilum ilaṅkiyiluṅkūṭa iruntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intiyāvil intak kaliyiruntatu eṉṟu colvataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ ituvari kiṭṭavilli. ataṉāl itaṉōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷa pommalāṭṭam, niḻalāṭṭam (pāvikkūttu) eṉṟa kalikaḷ intiyāviṟkuriyaṉavalla eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. yār eṉṉa kūṟiṉum cāvakattil nilaviya pāvikkūttu intiya mūlatti uṭiyatu eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli. ēṉeṉil irāmakātiyum pārata itikācamum iciyum itaṟku uyirāka iruppati niṉikkum poḻutu namatu uḷḷak kiṭakki cari eṉpati meyppippatāka irukkiṟatu. eṉavē intiyarkaḷ intiyāviliruntu cāvakattiṟkup puttam, civam ākiya camayaṅkaḷōṭu iciyiyum koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. irāmā yaṇam pāratam pōṉṟa itikācaṅkaḷiyum iti viḷakkum ciṟpakkaliyiyum, pommalāṭṭam eṉṟa putukkaliyiyum koṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatil viyappaṭivataṟ koṉṟum illi. aṟiñar āṉanta kumāracāmi paṇṭik kālattil intiyāvil pāvikkūttukkaḷ (pommalāṭṭaṅkaḷ) iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷ illi eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. avar tam karutti ēṟkum aṟiñar ji. cēkkap eṉpavariṉ karuttiyum mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟār. cāvaka niḻaṟ paṭaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tōliṉāl ceyyappaṭṭu iyaṅkakkūṭiya karaṅkaḷ, kālkaḷ, kaḻuttukkaḷ iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl avikaḷ cīṉāvip pōl oḷivīcukiṟatilli. parmā, tāylāntu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil ellāk kāṭcikaḷum iyaṟki nilakkāṭci yōṭu oṉṟittu aciya muṭiyātatāy amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. cāvaka niḻaṟpaṭam uruvaṅkaḷ kuṟippukkaḷōṭu kiyiṉāl iyakkappaṭu kiṟatu. niccayamākap pāvikkūttu oru poḻutu pōkku viḷi yāṭṭu eṉpati viṭa uyarntatāka matikkappaṭak kūṭiyatu ākum. paḻiya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ apimāṉattil iḵtu ōr oḻuṅku muṟi yāṉa caṭaṅkāka irukkiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ uṇarcci tōlpommi yākap piratipalikkiṟatu. uṇmiyāṉa pommalāṭṭam (Wayang Golek) kikaḷum kālkaḷum naṉṟākavum iyaṅkak kūṭiyaṉavākap parmāvil illāta vitamāy uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṟutiyāka, uyiruṭaṉ irukkum naṭikarkaḷ paṅku koḷḷum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷum kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. mukamūṭi viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ (Wayang Topeng) uyarnta paḻimic ciṉṉamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. oḻuṅkāṉa araṅkaṅkaḷiṉ āṭṭaṅkaḷ (Wayang Wong) niḻaṟpaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ (pommalāṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ) pōliviḷiyāṭṭākum. inta māṉuṣīka araṅkam mukkiyamāka eṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ uyar kulattiṉarāl uṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭatākum. āṉāl itaṉ tuṟikaḷ ellām paḻiya mūlaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu uruvāṉavi. pirapuk kaḷiṉ āṭikaḷ, kāṭcikaḷ, parampari nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ, apinayaṅkaḷ ellām toṉmiyāṉa mukattōṟṟattilēyē nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. intap paḻam poruḷkaḷ ellām varalāṟṟu rītiyāka illā viṭṭālum niccayamāka uḷḷattāl nēraṭiyāka uṇarappaṭum uṇmiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cāvaka araṅkaṅkaḷil itu uyir uḷḷatum uṇarccivacamāka aṟivuṟuttum vīrattiyum kātaliyum eṭuttukkāṭṭum paṭamākavum irukkiṉṟatu. niliyāṉa naṭikar kuḻukkaḷ yōkkiyakarttāvum curakarttāvum aracaviyāl ātarikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. aracakuṭumpattil uḷḷavarkaḷ kūṭa itil aṅkam  peṟṟiruntaṉar. cila mukkiya campavaṅkaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa naṭikarkaḷ mātak kaṇakkāyp payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭu vantaṉar. cāvaka araṅkaṅkaḷ ātmīka uṇarcciyiyum kalip paṇpiyum vaḷarkkum uyariya ciṉṉaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉa. itaṉōṭu cappāṉi luḷḷa nōkākku (No - gaku) oṉṟē oppiṭaṟkuriyatākum.
cāvakattiṉ nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ nalla araṅkil naṭipeṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟappāka naṭikarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyat taṉmi araṅkiṉmītu mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟum poḻutē kāṭṭappaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu. itu tavira araca camūkattil āṭuvataṟkāka irukkum peṇkaḷ cerimpil, peṭōyō nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷi āṭuvatil kitērntavar kaḷāka irukkiṟārkaḷ. aṉēkamāka araṇmaṉi nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ aṉittum alaṅkāra vakiyākavē irukkum apinayaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum muṟikaḷ ellām tamiḻakap pāṇiyit taḻuviyaṉa vākavē irukkum. paḻiya cilikaḷip piratipalikkum muṟiyil āṭum nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷ kuṟivākavē irukkum.
cāvaka nāṭṭut tēcīyap pāṇiyil uḷḷa pañcāṭi pāṭṭik (Batik) eṉpatu tamiḻaka mūlatti uṭiyatu eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamiṉṟu. perumpālāṉa āṭikaḷiṉ alaṅkāra vēlip pāṭukaḷ cāvakattil ilāmiya āṭci eḻumuṉ iruntaviyē yākum. ēṉiyavikaḷ ciṟappāka vaṭṭamākap pūvēli ceyyap paṭṭavikaḷākum. avikaḷ cāṇṭicivō veḷiccuvarkaḷmītu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pūvēlippāṭukaḷip piratipalippatāka iruntaṉa. mattiya cāvakattil nīlam, ūtā (Brown) ākiya iruniṟaṅkaḷ maṭṭum kāṇappaṭum. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil civappum pacciyum cērntirukkum. cātāraṇat tuṇṭukaḷ keyṉ pañcāṅ eṉa aḻikkappaṭum. ivikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu āṇkaḷ aṇiyum āṭi pōṉṟu irukkum. itik keyṉ cilaṇṭāṅ eṉpārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ mārpiṉmītu aṇiyum tāvaṇiyākavum upayōkap paṭuttappaṭum. taliyil kaṭṭappaṭum oru caturat tuṇiyik keyṉ kappālā eṉpārkaḷ. itu talip pākipōl maṭittut taliyil aṇiyappaṭum. intat talippākiyic cāvakattil taliyil iṟukap piṇittiruppārkaḷ. malēyāvilum, mēṟkuc cāvakatti lum cāram eṉṉum ari āṭiyi āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṇivar. pālit tīvil pakaṭṭāṉa āṭikaḷ (keyṉ praṭā) iḷavaracikaḷālum naṭikikaḷālum aṇiyappaṭum. cāvaka āṭikaḷ (paṭṭirik) poṟcarikiyiṉāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruppatōṭu accum aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum. inta āṭiyiṉ vēlippāṭukaḷ cuttamāṉa acal intiyap pāṇiyākavē ilaṅkum. orucila āṭiyiṉ alaṅkāra muṟitāṉ cīṉattit taḻuvi irukkum paṭṭu mikak kuṟivākavē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu.
 iḵtaṉṟi aḻakiya pañcāṭikaḷ cumattirā tīviṉ ṭōpar pāṭṭāk eṉṟa iṭattil piṟpōkkuḷḷa cila iṉattavarkaḷāl neyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pōrṉiyōvilum iṉṉumpala ciṟu tīvukaḷi lum pañcāṭikaḷ aḻakāka neyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷ ellām intiya pārampariyattuvam uṭiyaṉaveṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ aṉiva rālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cāvaka makaḷir mukaccāyalum naṭiyum āṭikaḷum nākarikamum paṇpum tamiḻakattōṭu mika neruṅki niṟpati iṉṟum kāṇalām. cāvakarkaḷ maṅkōli marapiṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭālum tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ mukac cāyal avarkaḷiṭam peritum piratipalikkiṟatu. uṭiyil naṭiyil uṇavil kuṇattil tamiḻc cāyal naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

12. muṭivuri
tamiḻakam, makkaḷ iṉattiṉ toṭṭil eṉṟār cermaṉ nāṭṭiṉ maṇṇūl aṟiñar hekkal avarkaḷ. `ulakil mikat toṉmiyāṉa moḻi tamiḻ, iṉṟum taṉ iḷami kuṉṟātu kaṉṉit tamiḻ, eḻil peṟṟu ilaṅkukiṟatu. immoḻi piṟamoḻi yiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkavallatu eṉṟu pukaḻmāli cūṭṭiyuḷḷār kiṟitava camayap periya kuruvākiya irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ. paḻantamiḻp pulavar kaṇiyaṉ pūṅkuṉṟaṉār avarkaḷ,
yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir	(puṟam. 192 : 1)
eṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
itaṉ mūlam, tamiḻakam ulaka makkaḷiṉ tāyakam; tamiḻ ulaka makkaḷukku urittāṉa toṉmi vāynta moḻi; tamiḻ makkaḷukku ulakaṉittum contam; ulakiṉar aṉivarum avarkaḷiṉ uṟaviṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.
inta eṇṇam - inta uṇarcci, tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam, aṟintō aṟiyāmalō uḷḷattil uṟintu kiṭakkiṟatu. eṉavē avarkaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē ulakiluḷḷa ellā iṭaṅkaḷi lum ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. kaṭal kaṭantu palvēṟu tīvukaḷilum pōy vāḻntaṉar. appāl moḻikaḷum, nāṭukaḷum pirintu, niṟamum, aṟamum, paṇpāṭukaḷum vēṟupaṭṭa piṉṉarum tamiḻarkaḷ palvēṟu nilappakutikaḷukkum ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. tīvukaḷilum, tīpakaṟpaṅkaḷilum pōy vāḻntaṉar. aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉar eṉṟa vuṇarvōṭu avarkaḷōṭu iṇintu cakōtara nēyattōṭu vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷōṭu koḷviṉi koṭuppiṉi ceytu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ camayattiyum kalikaḷiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum iṇittu nallaṟam pēṇum nāṉila makkaḷāy vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭipukunta nāṭuka ḷilellām taṅkaḷ camayattiyum kalikaḷiyum paṇpāṭṭi yum parappiṉar.
kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ palavaṟṟil  kiṟitava cakāptam tuḷippataṟku muṉṉarē intiyarkaḷ - vaṅkarkaḷ, kaliṅkarkaḷ, āntirarkaḷ, kucarāttiyarkaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu ulaka varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷuḷ perumpālōr tirāviṭap peruṅ kuṭimakkaḷ ēṉ? avarkaḷilum ciṟappumikkavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷē. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi ārāyntavarkaḷ avarkaḷ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē naṉiciṟanta nākarikattip paṭittavarkaḷ; kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil ki tērntavarkaḷ; āḻkaṭalil aḻakiya marak kalaṅkaḷil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kal toliviṟkuc ceṉṟu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷik kaṇṭavarkaḷ. ñāyiṟu oḷiyiṉik kāṇātu marañ ceṭi koṭi mutaliyavikaḷ aṭarntu putarkaḷāyk kāṭci aḷitta kāṭukaḷiyellām  kaṇṭu, kāṭukaḷintu nāṭukaḷākkiyum vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭiyum, kaḻaṉikaḷākat tiruttiyum putiya nākarikaṅkaḷi uruvākki yavarkaḷ. kiṟitu piṟappataṟku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē moḻikaḷiyum ataṟku varivaṭivaṅkaḷiyum vakuttavarkaḷ; nāṭu peyarntu cellat terintavarkaḷ eṉṟellām kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum tirāviṭarkaḷē ātiyil cumēriya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟku vantu cintu āṟṟiṉ arukē kuṭiyēṟi aiyāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉṉar mokañcatārō, arappā eṉṉum nakaraṅkaḷi eḻuppip paḻamperum nākarikaṅkaḷi vaḷarttavarkaḷ eṉṟu cintu natippakutiyil nilatti akaḻntu ārāyntu kaṇṭa airōppiyap pēraṟiñarkaḷ muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. cumēriya nāṭṭiluḷḷa `uruk eṉṟa iṭattil akaḻntu kaṇṭa pala poruḷkaḷukkum cintu veḷiyiṟ kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷukkum atikamāṉa orumippāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉmūlam toṉmiyāṉa kālattil `uruk nākarikatti uruvākkak kāraṇamāka irunta cumēriyarkaḷē intiyā pōntu intiyāviliruntu paṇṭiya makkaḷōṭu kalantu vāḻntu vanta iṉattavarāka irukka vēṇṭum. antak kalappiṉ vaḻivanta makkaḷē cintuveḷi makkaḷāvār eṉṟu aṟiñar mekkēy avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.1
cumēriya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu paluccitāṉam, arappā, mokañca tārō mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷē tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar.2 ivarkaḷ piṟkālattil āriyat tirāviṭap pōri ṉālō allatu iyaṟkik kōḷāṟiṉālō aṅkiruntu peyarntu paṭippaṭiyākap pallāṇṭākat teṟku nōkkit tirumpi vantaṉar. ivarkaḷil cilar āṅkāṅkē taṅki nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷi amittu vāḻntu vantaṉar. oru cilar teṟku nōkki vantu teṉkōṭiyil taṅkiṉar. ivarkaḷiyē iṉṟu nām tamiḻar eṉkiṟōm.
camayam vantāl ivarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu nāṭu viṭṭu nāṭu ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟum paṇpu vāyntavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ evvitamāṉa taṭpaveppanilikaḷiyum poṟuttuk koṇṭu vāḻat terinta varkaḷ. āyiram  āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka uyarnta oppaṟṟa nākarikatti vaḷarttu vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cumēriya nāṭṭil uruvākkiya nākarikattiviṭat taliciṟanta nākarikatti arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum uruvākkiyavarkaḷ. appāl ivarkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟi aṅku arappā, mokañcatārō nākarikattiviṭac ciṟanta nākarikatti oppaṟṟa nākarittik kaṭṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷ. intap paṇpu vāynta makkaḷ īḻam, malēyā, kaṭāram, ciṅkapuram, tāylāntu, kampōcakam, campā, pōrṉiyō, pāli, cāvakam, cumattirā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilellām pōntu ulaka mētikaḷ kaṇṭu viyakkum oppaṟṟa nākarikatti uruvākkiya varkaḷ. iyaṟkiyiṉ kōpattāl ivarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷil vaḷartta naṉiciṟanta nākarikaṅkaḷ iṉṟu nalivuṟṟukkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. pallāṇṭākat tamiḻ nāṭṭil eḻunta araciyal viḷivukaḷāl tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ iṉattavarkaḷ vāḻum cāvakam pōṉṟa nāṭ ṭōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koḷḷa muṭiyāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu.
tamiḻarkaḷ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi irukkiṉṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ kuṭiyēṟṟam māṇpu mikkatu. ulakil, tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭukaḷil cāvakam taliciṟanta kuṭiyēṟṟa mākum. avarkaḷ anta nāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟi annāṭṭu makkaḷōṭu pālil cīṉi karivatupōl iraṇṭaṟa iṇintuḷḷārkaḷ. ataṉōṭu taṅkaḷi annāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ moḻi, kali, paṇpāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu iṇittuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. iṉṟu cāvakattil vāḻum makkaḷil yār annāṭṭiṉ paḻamiyāṉa kuṭikaḷ? yār tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ? eṉṟu evarālum pirittuk kāṇamuṭiyātavāṟu oṉṟittu vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. atēpōṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kali etu? tamiḻ nāṭṭu moḻi etu? tamiḻnāṭṭu nākarikam etu? eṉṟu pirittuk kāṇa muṭiyātavāṟu ellām cāvakattil iṇintuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē tāṉ, ṭākṭar āṉantak kumāracuvāmi pōṉṟa kaliviṟpaṉṉarkaḷ cāvakattil oḷirvatu intiyak kaliyeṉṟō intōṉēciyak kaliyeṉṟō intō - cāvakak kaliyeṉṟō kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. eṉavē nām cāvakattil vāḻum makkaḷi intō cāvaka makkaḷ eṉṟum aṅkuk kāṇum kalikaḷi intō cāvakak kali eṉṟō allatu tirāviṭa cāvakak kali eṉṟō kūṟalām.
iṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa teḷivāṉa muṭivu cāvakattil vāḻum makkaḷil perumpālōr tamiḻarkaḷōṭu iratta campantamāṉa toṭarpuṭiya makkaḷākum. avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ ellām civa camayattit taḻuviyavarkaḷāvārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kalikaḷ ellām appaḻukkaṟṟa tamiḻnāṭṭuk  kalikaḷiṉ piratipalippēyākum. oru kālattil cāvakattil tamiḻ aracāṅka moḻiyākak kūṭa iruntatu eṉṟu palar eṇṇukiṉṟārkaḷ. iṉṟum aṅkuḷḷōrkaḷiṟ cilar pallavar kuṭumpam, pāṇṭiyar kuṭumpam, cōḻar kuṭumpam, cērar kuṭumpam eṉṟu colli varukiṟārkaḷ.1 maturi pōṉṟa ūrkaḷum, caracuvatiyāṟu pōṉṟa natikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ, vaṭamoḻic cācaṉaṅkaḷ pala mēlum mēlum kiṭittu varukiṉṟaṉa. palvēṟu vitamāṉa kōyilkaḷum, niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum, civaperumāṉ, civaliṅkam, pārvati, koṟṟavi, tirumāl, makāviṣṇu, cattiyapāmā, urukkumaṇi, nāṉmukam, akattiyar (civakuru), kaṇapati, cūriyaṉ, varuṇaṉ, vāyu, kupēraṉ, irāvaṇaṉ, irāmaṉ, cīti pōṉṟa tiru uruvaṅkaḷ pala kallilum, cempilum, veḷḷiyilum poṉṉilum kiṭittuḷ ḷaṉa. tamiḻar kaṇṭa nāṭṭiyattiṉ māṟṟuvaṭivu cāvakattilum kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.2 peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum paṇṭiyat tamiḻarkaḷip pōla nēṟṟu vari ariyil āṭikaḷ aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkip pōṉṟa tiruviḷakkukaḷ aṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. annāṭṭu āṇ peṇ makkaḷ tamiḻar kaḷip pōl aḻakum cāntamum poṟumiyum vāyntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṟ palar iṉṟu puttarkaḷāyum, ilāmiyarkaḷāyum iruntālum tamiḻar paṇṭipāṭṭiṉiṉṟu māṟātavarkaḷāy nalla tamiḻarkaḷ pōl iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. ituvari tamiḻarkaḷākiya nām namatu uṟaviṉarkaḷāṉa cāvaka makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷā malum avarkaḷ nāṭṭiluḷḷa civa neṟiyiyum avarkaḷiṉ kōyil teyvaṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷiyum kaṇṭu kaḷittu ārāyāma lum iruntuviṭṭōm. iṉiyāvatu tamiḻarkaḷ cāvaka nāṭṭiṟkuc camātāṉat tūtu cellavum kalit toṭarpu koḷḷavum araciyal naṭpi vaḷarkkavum muṉvara vēṇṭum.

1.	In Sumatra are found certin names of tribal subdivisions which are unmistakably South Indian, and specially Tamilian names such as Cholia Pandia Maelayala and also Pallava as well as Tekan (or Tekkanam or Deccan)- Early Tamil cultural influences in South East Asia S. J. Gunasegaran M.A. (Lond.)
2.	Java has had continuous contact with South India in later times. The Chola Empire in the 10th and the 11th centuries had close association with Java and Javenese culture was further influenced by Tamil Culture after the Cholas defeated the Sailendras of Java. Bhikkhus from Kanchipuram prise the Javanese ruler Hayam Wuruk in the 14th century Jayanagara adopted the characteristic Pandyan title Sundarapandya at his coronation in the 14th century and adopted the Pandyan emblem of the two fishes for his seal. - Early Tamil Cultural influences in South East Asia by S.J. Gunasegaran M.A. (Lond.) 1958- P. 14.
1.	“Some tribes among the head - hunting Dyaks in the wilds of Sumatra still call themselves Pandya, Chola Pellava and Maleyala. The great Sailendra rulers of Java and Sumatra who ruled a vast maritime empire from the seventh to the thirteenth Centuries with their Capitals at Srivijaya, modern palembang in Sumatra and Kadaram (Kedeah) to the north of Penang had the double - fish for their emblem like Pandyas of the extreme South of India.” Indian influence in the far East. by Prof. K.A. Neelakanta Sastri M.A. Page. 22
2.	“More extensive evidences of Indian Culture are found in middle of Java in the seventh century. This development may have been the result of long continued or of renewal immigration from South India” - History of India and Indonesian Art - by Ananda Coomaraswamy.

1.	Western Java remined independent and little is known of Central Java, but Majapahit controlled all eastern Islands, the Coastlands of Borneo, the Coastlands of Sumatra including Palembang and Malay Peninsula. Trade with China Indian Javanese products chiefly silk and cotton goods continued to flourish. In the Nagarakertagama Parapanca presents a vivid pricture of the walled city of Majapathit with its streets and palaces and of the manners and customs of its inhabitants. Entertinments are mentioned amongst others the Wayang Berber (exhibition of Scrol pintings with spoken text, and equivalent of the old Indian Yamapata exhibition as discribed in the Mudrarksasa) and Wayang Topeng, or masked dance, in which the King himself took part on the occasion of Straddha for the queen mother - History of Indian & Indonesian art - Ananda K. 	Borobudur sculptures are supreme in rendering the Buddhist ideal of mental repose, those of Parambanan picture the heroic deeds of the divine Rama with great vigour and perfect lucidity. 1.	The Cumulative evidence which we have been able to adduce points to Southern India as the homeland of Indo - Javanese Culture. The inscriptions however mentioned in this Connection are all Brahmanical. If now we turn to the two earliest Buddhist inscriptions known to exist in Java it is surprising to find that they are written in a Character which has been described as an early type of Nagari and which decidedly originates from Northern India. Are we then to assume that the Hindu emigrants who introduced Buddhism into Java came from North, as the promoters of Brahmanism in all likelihood hiled from the South of the Indian contenent? - The Relation between the Art of Indian and Java - Dr. J.Ph. Vogel. London. 1925.

A young Javanese Scholar attached to the Leiden University has the very appropriate name of Sastrovidaghda - i.e., Skt.
